# Attribution - sakuhart cat tile pack

2969 photographs from the **Open Images V7** dataset (Google), every one
licensed **CC BY 2.0** (https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/2.0/).

Each was cropped to a square and resized to 120px. For all but
**168** of them that square holds the whole box drawn around the
cat without cutting into it, and the box is at least
17% of the photograph's short side. The remaining 168 are
close-ups: the square is the detected face, so it does cut into the box.
Nothing else was changed.

CC BY 2.0 asks for credit to the photographer when an image is redistributed.
**If you publish a mosaic made from these photos, this file goes with it.**

- Photographs: **2969**
- Photographers: **1543**
